    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`

END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))
GROUP BY `h`.`department`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE

END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))
GROUP BY `h`.`department`

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))
GROUP BY `h`.`department`

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`

GROUP BY `h`.`department`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))

SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))
GROUP BY `h`.`department`

    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`

LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))

    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`
WHERE ((`h0`.`attendance_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`h0`.`attendance_date` <= @__end_1)) AND (`e`.`evaluation_id` IS NULL OR ((`e`.`evaluation_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`e`.`evaluation_date` <= @__end_1)))
GROUP BY `h`.`department`

SELECT `h`.`department`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Late' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalLateEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Present' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalPresentEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `deparmentTotalUndertimeEmployee`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Off Duty' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOffDuty`, COUNT(DISTINCT (CASE
    WHEN `h0`.`status` = 'Overtime' THEN `h`.`employee_id`
END)) AS `departmentTotalOvertime`, COALESCE(AVG(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN `e`.`average_score`
END), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationAverageScore`, COALESCE(COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `e`.`evaluation_id` IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(`e`.`total_score` AS decimal(65,30))
END), 0.0), 0.0) AS `departmentEvaluationTotalScore`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
LEFT JOIN `evaluations` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`evaluatee_id`

GROUP BY `h`.`department`

END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`
